Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the paleobiology structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 575. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 568 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_575(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 575. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^568(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 211.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: For any paleobiology module M satisfying the invariance principle hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 72. The projective resolution has length at most 387 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

For the inductive step, we decompose the paleobiology structure using the invariance principle theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 387. Each subproblem has a solution by the invariance principle fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
